# Documentation Index

Updated: 2026-06-07 CST

## Status Legend

This index mixes current entrypoints, active evidence, generated references, and historical handoffs. Do not infer authority from file age or from being listed near the top.

| Status | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `CURRENT_AUTHORITY` | Current entrypoint or rule source; read first before planning or editing. |
| `ACTIVE_EVIDENCE` | Useful evidence for a bounded lane; verify live paths/status before acting. |
| `VERIFY_BEFORE_USE` | Generated reference or dated map; use only after checking current code/runtime. |
| `HISTORICAL_EVIDENCE` | Past handoff/report/log; useful for context, not current authority. |
| `DEPRECATED_OR_ARCHIVED` | Old plan, old UI/PCUI route, old watchdog route, or archived script lane; do not resume without a current SSOT promoting it. |

## Historical Document Policy

Most root-level dated Markdown files are evidence packs or handoffs. Keep them for audit history, but do not rewrite them to match current state unless a future task explicitly asks for archival cleanup.

When an old document says one of the following, the 2026-05-18 current overlay wins:

- `Canary 5 -> Mini 50 -> Pilot 500 -> Production 93K`
- `Stage8 plan only / not implemented`
- `Stage9 plan only / not implemented`
- Qdrant/Neo4j/PC DB production ingestion implied as available
- Dajiala broad batch implied as acceptable without wave gates
- weekly activity `0 discovered` implied as no new posts despite invalid session
- old `NEXT_AGENT_HANDOFF_*`, root `HANDOFF_*`, `*_PLAN_*`, `NIGHT_WATCHER_LOG_*`, and `docs\atlas-*-handoff-*` files describe a dated checkpoint unless this index, the thread router, or `docs\current-runtime.md` explicitly promotes them
- LDR prompt/status files with old "execute now" or "repaired" wording are sidecar evidence only; T6 execution still requires a current request and non-secret provider-state check
